# Near misses and US drafts

C that is not linked and not counted: a starting point. One file per function,
`nonmatching/<program>/func_XXXXXXXX.c` (`<program>` is `frontbin`, `boot_elf`, `i5bootn` or `levels`,
the name is the PAL address), with a comment at its top saying where it came from and what still
differs. Move it into `src/` once the full build prints `MATCH` with it in place, and delete its
row here. `tests/check_repository.py` checks that every file has a row.

A draft that uses inline asm (beyond `.extern` size hints), `volatile` (beyond named hardware memory and the literal EE hardware register and scratchpad addresses AGENTS.md allows), a `#define` alias or a hardcoded address is a study aid, not a route to a match: those are not allowed in matched C, so such a function stays here until plain C matches.

Files that start with `/* US draft` are rac3-uya-decomp's C for that function (commit 13cf5c5),
with names moved to PAL addresses where the address maps are sure of them
(`tools/port_from_us.py`, docs/history/porting-from-us.md). They were not carried for the reason in their row:

- **flags**: the US match used flags of its own for this one function (a "single-function
  override" or `@ps2as` range), which this repository does not allow (docs/build/build-fidelity.md);
- **differs**: the PAL function's bytes are not the US ones, or the C did not build to them here;
- **symbols**: a name in the C has no PAL address yet.

`functional.tsv` lists the US drafts OpenRAC's native port of NTSC-U reads in place of the
assembly (`tools/us_names.py --candidates`, docs/build/versions.md): C that matched NTSC-U, for a
function NTSC-U has and src/us/ has no C for.

| `func_L32_003A1E58` | levels | Sonnet worker b10-u1 (2026-11-18) | 5 diff with `-mvu0-use-vf0-vf2` + `--as ps2as` (the file's line has neither; its other blocks not re-checked under them). Everything but one scheduling slot is in: the loop with the p++ / j += 4 pair (explicit j gave retail's `addu $v0,$s4,$s6`), the integer sum `(s32)(*p << 7) + (s32)D_L32_001DFF00` for the first and the sub_ba row (retail's index-first addu), the three float tests and the take block. Two causes: (1) retail's `sll $a0,$a0,3` is in place (the pseudo shares $a0) and the address is `lui; addiu` then `sll`, this C emits lui, sll, addiu (sched2 tie by LUID: the sll pseudo is created before the symbol's high/lo_sum). `arg &= 0xFF; arg <<= 3; t = (u8 *)&D_00160C40; *(s32 *)(t + arg + 0x5fac)` gives lui, addiu, in-place sll (right) but the base then lands in $v0 and the sum is `addu $v1,$a0,$v0` (6 diffs again); `D` as a scalar `u8` with `.extern D, 64` folds 0x5fac into the lui/addiu pair; a pointer local in each arm gives right order but wrong registers (8-9); -mno-split-addresses 15; idx locals, ternary, a-first (7-12). |

A plain 128-bit access cannot fold a %lo() address; an asm "m" operand for both accesses of the first slot can, but then the `lui` becomes the first insn of the tail and reorg copies it into the delay slot of every switch arm's `b` (retail: `nop`; 258 raw).
